220|     (function () {
221|         'use strict';
222| 
223|         // Lecture des media queries CSS (vous les modifiez dans le CSS)
224|         const desktopMQ = window.matchMedia('(min-width: 1081px)');
225|         const mobileMQ = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 350px)');
226|         // Entre 351px et 1080px = mode desktop également
227| 
228|         // === VERSION DESKTOP ===
229|         function initDesktopVersion() {
230|             const nav = document.getElementById('mobileNav');
231|             const panel = document.getElementById('navSettingsPanel');
232|             const settingsBtn = document.getElementById('navSettingsBtn');
233|             const navButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.mobile-nav button:not(#toggleNavBtn)');
234| 
235|             let isPanelOpen = false;
236|             let animationTimer = null;
237| 
238|             if (nav) nav.style.display = 'flex';
239| 
240|             // Bouton bleu
241|             const oldToggleBtn = document.getElementById('toggleNavBtn');
242|             if (oldToggleBtn) {
243|                 const newToggleBtn = oldToggleBtn.cloneNode(true);
244|                 oldToggleBtn.parentNode.replaceChild(newToggleBtn, oldToggleBtn);
245|                 newToggleBtn.onclick = function (e) {
246|                     e.preventDefault();
247|                     const isVisible = navButtons[0]?.classList.contains('hidden');
248|                     navButtons.forEach(btn => {
249|                         if (isVisible) btn.classList.remove('hidden');
250|                         else btn.classList.add('hidden');
251|                     });
252|                     localStorage.setItem('mobileNavVisible', !isVisible);
253|                 };
254|             }
255| 
256|             function setPanelOrientation() {
257|                 if (!panel || !nav) return;
258|                 const pos = nav.getAttribute('data-position') || 'bottom';
259|                 panel.classList.remove('horizontal', 'vertical');
260|                 if (pos === 'left' || pos === 'right') {
261|                     panel.classList.add('vertical');
262|                 } else {
263|                     panel.classList.add('horizontal');
264|                 }
265|             }
266| 
267|             function positionPanel() {
268|                 if (!panel || !nav) return;
269|                 const navRect = nav.getBoundingClientRect();
270|                 const panelW = panel.offsetWidth;
271|                 const panelH = panel.offsetHeight;
272|                 const pos = nav.getAttribute('data-position') || 'bottom';
273|                 if (panelW === 0 || panelH === 0) return;
274| 
275|                 let top, left;
276|                 switch (pos) {
277|                     case 'left':
278|                         left = navRect.right + 8;
279|                         top = navRect.top + (navRect.height / 2) - (panelH / 2);
280|                         break;
281|                     case 'right':
282|                         left = navRect.left - panelW - 8;
283|                         top = navRect.top + (navRect.height / 2) - (panelH / 2);
284|                         break;
285|                     case 'top':
286|                         left = navRect.left;
287|                         top = navRect.bottom + 8;
288|                         break;
289|                     default:
290|                         left = navRect.left;
291|                         top = navRect.top - panelH - 8;
292|                         break;
293|                 }
294| 
295|                 if (left < 5) left = 5;
296|                 if (left + panelW > window.innerWidth - 5) left = window.innerWidth - panelW - 5;
297|                 if (top < 5) top = 5;
298|                 if (top + panelH > window.innerHeight - 5) {
299|                     if (pos === 'top') top = navRect.top - panelH - 8;
300|                     else if (pos === 'bottom') top = navRect.bottom + 8;
301|                     else top = window.innerHeight - panelH - 5;
302|                 }
303| 
304|                 panel.style.top = top + 'px';
305|                 panel.style.left = left + 'px';
306|             }
307| 
308|             function openPanel() {
309|                 if (!panel) return;
310|                 panel.style.display = 'flex';
311|                 isPanelOpen = true;
312|                 setPanelOrientation();
313|                 positionPanel();
314|             }
315| 
316|             function closePanel() {
317|                 if (!panel) return;
318|                 panel.style.display = 'none';
319|                 isPanelOpen = false;
320|                 if (animationTimer) clearTimeout(animationTimer);
321|             }
322| 
323|             function togglePanel(e) {
324|                 e.stopPropagation();
325|                 if (isPanelOpen) closePanel();
326|                 else openPanel();
327|             }
328| 
329|             function setNavPosition(position) {
330|                 if (!nav) return;
331|                 nav.setAttribute('data-position', position);
332|                 localStorage.setItem('mobileNavPosition', position);
333|                 if (isPanelOpen) {
334|                     if (animationTimer) clearTimeout(animationTimer);
335|                     animationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
336|                         setPanelOrientation();
337|                         positionPanel();
338|                         animationTimer = null;
339|                     }, 320);
340|                 }
341|             }
342| 
343|             function loadSavedSettings() {
344|                 const savedPos = localStorage.getItem('mobileNavPosition');
345|                 if (savedPos && ['top', 'bottom', 'left', 'right'].includes(savedPos)) {
346|                     nav.setAttribute('data-position', savedPos);
347|                 } else {
348|                     nav.setAttribute('data-position', 'bottom');
349|                 }
350|                 const savedVis = localStorage.getItem('mobileNavVisible');
351|                 if (savedVis === 'true') navButtons.forEach(btn => btn.classList.add('hidden'));
352|                 else if (savedVis === 'false') navButtons.forEach(btn => btn.classList.remove('hidden'));
353|             }
354| 
355|             function resetAll() {
356|                 closePanel();
357|                 localStorage.removeItem('mobileNavPosition');
358|                 localStorage.removeItem('mobileNavVisible');
359|                 location.reload();
360|             }
361| 
362|             function handleClickOutside(e) {
363|                 if (!isPanelOpen) return;
364|                 if (!nav.contains(e.target) && !panel.contains(e.target)) closePanel();
365|             }
366| 
367|             function scrollToTarget(e) {
368|                 const btn = e.currentTarget;
369|                 const targetId = btn.getAttribute('data-target');
370|                 if (!targetId) return;
371|                 e.preventDefault();
372|                 const target = document.getElementById(targetId);
373|                 if (target) {
374|                     closePanel();
375|                     const offset = nav.getAttribute('data-position') === 'top' ? nav.offsetHeight + 20 : 80;
376|                     const pos = target.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY - offset;
377|                     window.scrollTo({ top: pos, behavior: 'smooth' });
378|                 }
379|             }
380| 
381|             if (nav && panel) {
382|                 loadSavedSettings();
383|                 if (settingsBtn) settingsBtn.onclick = togglePanel;
384|                 document.getElementById('navPosTop')?.addEventListener('click', () => setNavPosition('top'));
385|                 document.getElementById('navPosBottom')?.addEventListener('click', () => setNavPosition('bottom'));
386|                 document.getElementById('navPosLeft')?.addEventListener('click', () => setNavPosition('left'));
387|                 document.getElementById('navPosRight')?.addEventListener('click', () => setNavPosition('right'));
388|                 document.getElementById('navReset')?.addEventListener('click', resetAll);
389|                 document.querySelectorAll('[data-target]').forEach(btn => {
390|                     btn.addEventListener('click', scrollToTarget);
391|                 });
392|                 document.addEventListener('click', handleClickOutside);
393|                 window.addEventListener('resize', () => { if (isPanelOpen) positionPanel(); });
394|             }
395|         }
396| 
397|         // === VERSION MOBILE (très petits écrans) ===
398|         function initMobileVersion() {
399|             const oldNav = document.getElementById('mobileNav');
400|             if (oldNav) oldNav.style.display = 'none';
401| 
402|             let menuBtn = document.getElementById('mobileMenuBtn');
403|             if (!menuBtn) {
404|                 menuBtn = document.createElement('button');
405|                 menuBtn.id = 'mobileMenuBtn';
406|                 menuBtn.innerHTML = '☰';
407|                 document.body.appendChild(menuBtn);
408|             }
409| 
410|             let panel = document.getElementById('mobileNavPanel');
411|             if (!panel) {
412|                 panel = document.createElement('div');
413|                 panel.id = 'mobileNavPanel';
414|                 panel.innerHTML = `
415|                 <div class="mobile-panel-buttons">
416|                     <button data-target="connectes">👥<span>${Translator.t('chat.connected_btn')}</span></button>
417|                     <button data-target="chat">💬<span>${Translator.t('chat.chat_btn')}</span></button>
418|                     <button data-target="new-window">📦<span>${Translator.t('chat.new_btn')}</span></button>
419|                     <button data-target="menus">📋<span>${Translator.t('chat.menus_btn')}</span></button>
420|                 </div>
421|             `;
422|                 document.body.appendChild(panel);
423|             }
424| 
425|             let isOpen = false;
426| 
427|             function openPanel() {
428|                 panel.classList.add('open');
429|                 isOpen = true;
430|             }
431| 
432|             function closePanel() {
433|                 panel.classList.remove('open');
434|                 isOpen = false;
435|             }
436| 
437|             function togglePanel() {
438|                 if (isOpen) closePanel();
439|                 else openPanel();
440|             }
441| 
442|             function scrollToTarget(e) {
443|                 const btn = e.currentTarget;
444|                 const targetId = btn.getAttribute('data-target');
445|                 if (!targetId) return;
446|                 e.preventDefault();
447|                 const target = document.getElementById(targetId);
448|                 if (target) {
449|                     const pos = target.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY - 70;
450|                     window.scrollTo({ top: pos, behavior: 'smooth' });
451|                 }
452|             }
453| 
454|             menuBtn.addEventListener('click', togglePanel);
455|             document.querySelectorAll('#mobileNavPanel [data-target]').forEach(btn => {
456|                 btn.addEventListener('click', scrollToTarget);
457|             });
458| 
459|             document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
460|                 if (isOpen && !panel.contains(e.target) && e.target !== menuBtn) {
461|                     closePanel();
462|                 }
463|             });
464|         }
465| 
466|         // === LANCEMENT (basé sur les media queries CSS) ===
467|         function detectAndInit() {
468|             if (mobileMQ.matches) {
469|                 initMobileVersion();
470|             } else {
471|                 initDesktopVersion();
472|             }
473|         }
474| 
475|         detectAndInit();
476| 
477|         // === REDIMENSIONNEMENT ===
478|         let resizeTimer;
479|         desktopMQ.addEventListener('change', function () {
480|             if (resizeTimer) clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
481|             resizeTimer = setTimeout(() => location.reload(), 150);
482|         });
483|         mobileMQ.addEventListener('change', function () {
484|             if (resizeTimer) clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
485|             resizeTimer = setTimeout(() => location.reload(), 150);
486|         });
487|     })();
488| 
489| })();

9| function playMessageSound() {
10|     const soundEnabled = localStorage.getItem('messageSoundEnabled') !== 'false';
11|     if (!soundEnabled) return;

13|     const audio = document.getElementById('messageSound');
14|     if (!audio) return;
15| 
16|     const volume = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('messageSoundVolume')) || 70;
17|     audio.volume = volume / 100;
18|     audio.currentTime = 0;
19|     audio.play().catch(() => { });
20| }

55| // Mettre à jour la liste des bloqués dans le panel
56| function updateBlockedList() {
57|     const list = document.getElementById('blockedList');
58|     const count = document.getElementById('blockedCount');
59|     if (!list) return;
60| 
61|     const blocked = window.blockedUsers || [];
62|     if (count) count.textContent = blocked.length;
63| 
64|     if (blocked.length === 0) {
65|         list.innerHTML = Translator.t('chat.no_blocked');
66|         return;
67|     }
68| 
69|     list.innerHTML = blocked.map(pseudo => `
70|         <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; padding: 3px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid #333;">
71|             <span>${escapeHtml(pseudo)}</span>
72|             <span style="color: #ff6b6b; cursor: pointer;" onclick="unblockUser('${pseudo}')">${Translator.t('chat.unblock_btn')}</span>
73|         </div>
74|     `).join('');
75| }
76| 
77| // Charger la liste des bloqués depuis la base
78| async function loadBlockedUsers() {
79|     console.log('🔍 loadBlockedUsers appelée, user.pseudo =', user?.pseudo);
80|     try {
81|         const response = await fetch('/api/blocked-list', {
82|             method: 'POST',
83|             headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
84|             body: JSON.stringify({ pseudo: user.pseudo })
85|         });
86|         const data = await response.json();
87|         console.log('📥 Réponse de /api/blocked-list:', data);
88|         if (data.success) {
89|             window.blockedUsers = data.blocked || [];
90|             console.log('🔒 Bloques chargés depuis la base:', window.blockedUsers);
91|             updateBlockedList();
92|             window.fetchActiveUsers();
93|         }
94|     } catch (err) {
95|         console.error('❌ Erreur chargement bloqués:', err);
96|         window.blockedUsers = [];
97|     }
98| }
99| 
100| // Bloquer un utilisateur
101| window.blockUser = async function (pseudo) {
102|     console.log('🔥 blockUser appelée pour', pseudo);
103| 
104|     if (pseudo === user.pseudo) {
105|         alert(Translator.t('chat.alert_cannot_block_self'));
106|         return;
107|     }
108| 
109|     if (window.blockedUsers.includes(pseudo)) {
110|         alert(`${pseudo} ${Translator.t('chat.alert_already_blocked')}`);
111|         return;
112|     }
113| 
114|     try {
115|         console.log('📤 Appel à /api/block avec:', { blocker: user.pseudo, blocked: pseudo });
116|         const response = await fetch('/api/block', {
117|             method: 'POST',
118|             headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
119|             body: JSON.stringify({ blocker: user.pseudo, blocked: pseudo })
120|         });
121|         const data = await response.json();
122|         console.log('📥 Réponse de /api/block:', data);
123| 
124|         if (data.success) {
125|             window.blockedUsers.push(pseudo);
126|             updateBlockedList();
127|             if (currentTargetName === pseudo) {
128|                 messageInput.disabled = true;
129|                 sendBtn.disabled = true;
130|                 messageInput.placeholder = `🔇 ${pseudo} ${Translator.t('chat.blocked_placeholder')}`;
131|             }
132|             window.fetchActiveUsers();
133|             console.log(`🔒 ${pseudo} bloqué`);
134|         } else {
135|             // 🔥 NOUVEAU : Même si l'API retourne une erreur "Déjà bloqué", on met à jour l'interface
136|             if (data.error === 'Déjà bloqué') {
137|                 if (!window.blockedUsers.includes(pseudo)) {
138|                     window.blockedUsers.push(pseudo);
139|                     updateBlockedList();
140|                     if (currentTargetName === pseudo) {
141|                         messageInput.disabled = true;
142|                         sendBtn.disabled = true;
143|                         messageInput.placeholder = `🔇 ${pseudo} ${Translator.t('chat.blocked_placeholder')}`;
144|                     }
145|                     window.fetchActiveUsers();
146|                     console.log(`🔒 ${pseudo} déjà bloqué (interface synchronisée)`);
147|                 }
148|             } else {
149|                 alert(data.error || Translator.t('chat.alert_block_error'));
150|             }
151|         }
152|     } catch (err) {
153|         console.error('❌ Erreur blocage:', err);
154|         alert(Translator.t('chat.alert_block_error'));
155|     }
156| };
157| 
158| // Débloquer un utilisateur
159| window.unblockUser = async function (pseudo) {
160|     console.log('🔥 unblockUser appelée pour', pseudo);
161|     try {
162|         const response = await fetch('/api/unblock', {
163|             method: 'POST',
164|             headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
165|             body: JSON.stringify({ blocker: user.pseudo, blocked: pseudo })
166|         });
167|         const data = await response.json();
168|         if (data.success) {
169|             const index = window.blockedUsers.indexOf(pseudo);
170|             if (index > -1) {
171|                 window.blockedUsers.splice(index, 1);
172|             }
173|             updateBlockedList();
174|             // Si c'était le contact actuel, réactiver l'envoi
175|             if (currentTargetName === pseudo) {
176|                 messageInput.disabled = false;
177|                 sendBtn.disabled = false;
178|                 messageInput.placeholder = `${Translator.t('chat.message_for')} ${pseudo}...`;
179|             }
180|             // Rafraîchir la liste des utilisateurs
181|             window.fetchActiveUsers();
182|             console.log(`🔓 ${pseudo} débloqué`);
183|         } else {
184|             alert(data.error || Translator.t('chat.alert_unblock_error'));
185|         }
186|     } catch (err) {
187|         console.error('Erreur déblocage:', err);
188|         alert(Translator.t('chat.alert_unblock_error'));
189|     }
190| };

199|     <div id="mg-modal-box" style="background: #1e1e2e; border-radius: 16px; padding: 24px; max-width: 380px; width: 85%; text-align: center; box-shadow: 0 20px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.3); border: 1px solid #333; font-family: system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif; transform: translateY(-50px); opacity: 0; transition: all 0.3s ease;">
200|         <h3 id="mg-modal-title" style="margin: 0 0 12px 0; color: #ff6b6b; font-size: 1.3rem;">⚠️ ${Translator.t('chat.modal_title')}</h3>
201|         <p id="mg-modal-message" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0; color: #ccc; font-size: 1rem; line-height: 1.5;">${Translator.t('chat.modal_default_message')}</p>

212|     function setupButtons(onConfirm, onCancel) {
213|         const confirmBtn = document.getElementById('mg-modal-confirm');
214|         const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('mg-modal-cancel');
215|         const container = document.getElementById('mg-modal-container');
216| 
217|         if (confirmBtn) {
218|             const newConfirm = confirmBtn.cloneNode(true);
219|             confirmBtn.parentNode.replaceChild(newConfirm, confirmBtn);
220|             newConfirm.onclick = () => {
221|                 container.style.display = 'none';
222|                 if (onConfirm) onConfirm();
223|             };
224|         }
225| 
226|         if (cancelBtn) {
227|             const newCancel = cancelBtn.cloneNode(true);
228|             cancelBtn.parentNode.replaceChild(newCancel, cancelBtn);
229|             newCancel.onclick = () => {
230|                 container.style.display = 'none';
231|                 if (onCancel) onCancel();
232|             };
233|         }
234| 
235|         container.onclick = (e) => {
236|             if (e.target === container) {
237|                 container.style.display = 'none';
238|                 if (onCancel) onCancel();
239|             }
240|         };
241|     }

243|     window.MGModal = {
244|         show: function (options) {
245|             createModalStructure();
246| 
247|             const container = document.getElementById('mg-modal-container');
248|             const box = document.getElementById('mg-modal-box');
249|             const titleEl = document.getElementById('mg-modal-title');
250|             const messageEl = document.getElementById('mg-modal-message');
251| 
252|             titleEl.textContent = options.title || Translator.t('chat.modal_title');
253|             messageEl.textContent = options.message || Translator.t('chat.modal_default_message');
254| 
255|             setupButtons(options.onConfirm, options.onCancel);
256| 
257|             container.style.display = 'flex';
258| 
259|             setTimeout(() => {
260|                 if (box) {
261|                     box.style.transform = 'translateY(0)';
262|                     box.style.opacity = '1';
263|                 }
264|             }, 10);
265|         },
266| 
267|         hide: function () {
268|             const container = document.getElementById('mg-modal-container');
269|             if (container) container.style.display = 'none';
270|         }
271|     };
272| })();

296| let reconnectAttempts = 0;
297| const MAX_RECONNECT_ATTEMPTS = 10;
298| const RECONNECT_DELAYS = [500, 1000, 2000, 4000, 8000, 16000, 30000, 30000, 30000, 30000];
299| 
300| function reconnect() {
301|     if (reconnectAttempts >= MAX_RECONNECT_ATTEMPTS) return;
302|     const delay = RECONNECT_DELAYS[reconnectAttempts] || 30000;
303|     reconnectAttempts++;
304|     setTimeout(() => {
305|         socket.connect();
306|     }, delay);
307| }
308| 
309| socket.on('disconnect', () => {
310|     reconnect();
311| });
312| 
313| socket.on('connect', () => {
314|     reconnectAttempts = 0;
315| });
316| 
317| // ========== GESTION DE LA RECONNEXION APRÈS VEILLE ==========
318| function checkAndReconnect() {
319|     if (socket) {
320|         if (!socket.connected) {
321|             console.log('🔄 Socket déconnecté, reconnexion forcée...');
322|             socket.connect();
323|             // Après reconnexion, vérifier les messages
324|             setTimeout(() => {
325|                 if (socket.connected) {
326|                     const pseudo = getCurrentPseudo();
327|                     if (pseudo) {
328|                         console.log('📨 Vérification des messages en attente après reconnexion forcée');
329|                         socket.emit('check-pending', { pseudo: pseudo });
330|                     }
331|                 }
332|             }, 1000);
333|         } else {
334|             // Si connecté, vérifier quand même (par sécurité)
335|             const pseudo = getCurrentPseudo();
336|             if (pseudo) {
337|                 console.log('📨 Vérification périodique des messages en attente');
338|                 socket.emit('check-pending', { pseudo: pseudo });
339|             }
340|         }
341|     }
342| }

344| // ========== AVERTISSEMENT D'INACTIVITÉ ==========
345| socket.on('inactivity-warning', (data) => {
346|     if (typeof MGModal !== 'undefined') {
347|         MGModal.show({
348|             title: Translator.t('chat.inactivity_title'),
349|             message: data.message || Translator.t('chat.inactivity_message'),
350|             onConfirm: () => {
351|                 // L'utilisateur a cliqué → réinitialiser
352|                 socket.emit('reset-inactivity');
353|             }
354|         });
355|     } else {
356|         // Fallback
357|         alert(data.message || Translator.t('chat.inactivity_message'));
358|         socket.emit('reset-inactivity');
359|     }
360| });

366|     }
367| };

440| // ========== RÉCUPÉRATION DES INFOS UTILISATEUR DEPUIS LA BASE ==========00000
441| let user = null;
442| let currentTargetName = null;
443| const storedUser = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('meetgay_user'));
444| 
445| async function initUser() {
446|     console.log('1. Début initUser');
447| 
448|     // 🔥 NOUVEAU : Vérifier si c'est un rechargement
449|     const isReloading = sessionStorage.getItem('meetgay_is_reloading') === 'true';
450|     if (isReloading) {
451|         console.log('🔄 Rechargement détecté, attente avant de continuer...');
452|         // Attendre que la page soit stabilisée
453|         await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 800));
454|         console.log('✅ Reprise après attente');
455|     }
456| 
457|     if (!storedUser || !storedUser.pseudo) {
458|         console.log('2. Pas de storedUser');
459|         // Ne rediriger que si ce n'est pas un rechargement
460|         if (!isReloading) {
461|             window.location.href = '/login.html';
462|         }
463|         return;
464|     }
465| 
466|     try {
467|         console.log('3. Appel fetch pour pseudo:', storedUser.pseudo);
468|         const response = await fetch('/api/get-user', {
469|             method: 'POST',
470|             headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
471|             body: JSON.stringify({ pseudo: storedUser.pseudo })
472|         });
473| 
474|         console.log('4. Réponse reçue, status:', response.status);
475|         const data = await response.json();
476|         console.log('5. Data reçue:', data);
477| 
478|         if (data.success) {
479|             user = data.user;
480|             currentUserPseudo = user.pseudo;
481|             localStorage.setItem('meetgay_user_full', JSON.stringify(user));
482|             console.log('6. Utilisateur chargé, démarrage chat');
483|             startChat();
484|         } else {
485|             console.log('7. Pas de success');
486|             // 🔥 SI C'EST UN RECHARGEMENT ET QUE L'UTILISATEUR N'EXISTE PAS
487|             if (isReloading) {
488|                 console.log('⚠️ Utilisateur non trouvé pendant rechargement - Tentative de récupération');
489|                 // Essayer de récupérer l'utilisateur via une autre méthode
490|                 try {
491|                     const reconnResponse = await fetch('/api/reconnect-user', {
492|                         method: 'POST',
493|                         headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
494|                         body: JSON.stringify({ pseudo: storedUser.pseudo })
495|                     });
496|                     const reconnData = await reconnResponse.json();
497|                     if (reconnData.success) {
498|                         user = reconnData.user;
499|                         currentUserPseudo = user.pseudo;
500|                         localStorage.setItem('meetgay_user_full', JSON.stringify(user));
501|                         console.log('✅ Utilisateur récupéré, démarrage chat');
502|                         startChat();
503|                         return;
504|                     }
505|                 } catch (e) {
506|                     console.error('Erreur reconnexion:', e);
507|                 }
508|             }
509|             // Si tout échoue, rediriger
510|             window.location.href = '/login.html';
511|         }
512|     } catch (err) {
513|         console.error('Erreur:', err);
514|         if (!isReloading) {
515|             window.location.href = '/login.html';
516|         }
517|     }
518| }

520| // ========== DÉMARRAGE DU CHAT (après récupération des infos) ==========
521| function startChat() {
522| 
523| 
524|     if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
525|         navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js').then(reg => {
526|             console.log('SW enregistré');
527|             if (reg.active) {
528|                 reg.active.postMessage({ action: 'userConnected', connected: true });
529|             } else {
530|                 reg.installing?.postMessage({ action: 'userConnected', connected: true });
531|             }
532|         });
533|     }
534|     // Charger l'historique des messages pour une conversation
535|     async function loadHistory(withPseudo) {
536|         try {
537|             const response = await fetch(`/api/messages/${encodeURIComponent(user.pseudo)}/${encodeURIComponent(withPseudo)}`);
538|             const messages = await response.json();
539| 
540|             // Nettoyer l'historique existant pour ce contact
541|             if (!conversationHistory[withPseudo]) {
542|                 conversationHistory[withPseudo] = [];
543|             } else {
544|                 conversationHistory[withPseudo] = [];
545|             }
546| 
547|             // Ajouter les messages chargés
548|             messages.forEach(msg => {
549|                 const type = msg.from_pseudo === user.pseudo ? 'sent' : 'received';
550|                 conversationHistory[withPseudo].push({
551|                     text: msg.message,
552|                     type: type,
553|                     from: msg.from_pseudo,
554|                     to: msg.to_pseudo,
555|                     timestamp: new Date(msg.created_at)
556|                 });
557|             });
558| 
559|             updateHistoryDisplay();
560|         } catch (err) {
561|             console.error('Erreur chargement historique:', err);
562|         }
563|     }
564| 
565|     const usersDiv = document.getElementById('usersList');
566|     const messageInput = document.getElementById('messageInput');
567|     const sendBtn = document.getElementById('sendBtn');
568|     const envelope = document.getElementById('envelope');
569| 
570| 
571|     // --------------------------------------------------------------
572|     // GESTION DE LA LISTE DES CONNECTÉS
573|     // --------------------------------------------------------------
574| 
575|     window.fetchActiveUsers = async function () {
576|         try {
577|             const response = await fetch('/api/users/active');
578|             const usersListData = await response.json();
579| 
580|             usersListData.sort((a, b) => (b.first_connected_at || 0) - (a.first_connected_at || 0));
581|             usersList = usersListData;
582| 
583|             const onlineCount = document.getElementById('onlineCount');
584|             if (onlineCount) {
585|                 onlineCount.textContent = usersList.length;
586|             }
587| 
588|             const usersDiv = document.getElementById('usersList');
589|             if (!usersDiv) return;
590| 
591|             // 🔥 Vider le contenu existant avant d'ajouter les utilisateurs
592|             usersDiv.innerHTML = '';
593| 
594|             if (usersList.length === 0) {
595|                 usersDiv.innerHTML = `<em>${Translator.t('chat.no_connected')}</em>`;
596|                 return;
597|             }
598| 
599|             // 🔥 Récupérer les pseudos actuellement affichés
600|             const currentUsers = new Set();
601|             usersDiv.querySelectorAll('.user-item').forEach(el => {
602|                 const pseudo = el.dataset.pseudo;
603|                 if (pseudo) currentUsers.add(pseudo);
604|             });
605| 
606|             const newUsers = new Set(usersList.map(u => u.pseudo));
607| 
608|             // 🔥 Supprimer les utilisateurs qui ne sont plus connectés
609|             usersDiv.querySelectorAll('.user-item').forEach(el => {
610|                 const pseudo = el.dataset.pseudo;
611|                 if (pseudo && !newUsers.has(pseudo)) {
612|                     el.remove();
613|                 }
614|             });
615| 
616|             // 🔥 Ajouter les nouveaux utilisateurs
617|             for (const fullUser of usersList) {
618|                 const existingItem = usersDiv.querySelector(`.user-item[data-pseudo="${fullUser.pseudo}"]`);
619|                 if (!existingItem) {
620|                     const div = document.createElement('div');
621|                     div.className = 'user-item';
622|                     div.dataset.pseudo = fullUser.pseudo;
623|                     const orientationSymbol = getOrientationSymbol(fullUser.tendencies);
624|                     const isBlocked = window.blockedUsers.includes(fullUser.pseudo);
625|                     const locationDisplay = fullUser.location_city || fullUser.location_code || '?';
626| 
627|                     // 🔥 Drapeau de langue
628|                     let languageFlag = '';
629|                     if (fullUser.language === 'fr') {
630|                         languageFlag = `<img src="/images/flags/fr.svg" class="flag-icon" alt="FR" style="width:16px;height:12px;vertical-align:middle;margin-left:4px;">`;
631|                     } else if (fullUser.language === 'nl') {
632|                         languageFlag = `<img src="/images/flags/nl.svg" class="flag-icon" alt="NL" style="width:16px;height:12px;vertical-align:middle;margin-left:4px;">`;
633|                     } else if (fullUser.language === 'both') {
634|                         languageFlag = `<img src="/images/flags/fr.svg" class="flag-icon" alt="FR" style="width:16px;height:12px;vertical-align:middle;margin-left:4px;"> <img src="/images/flags/nl.svg" class="flag-icon" alt="NL" style="width:16px;height:12px;vertical-align:middle;">`;
635|                     } else {
636|                         languageFlag = '❓';
637|                     }
638| 
639|                     if (fullUser.pseudo === user.pseudo) {
640|                         div.innerHTML = `<strong>${escapeHtml(fullUser.pseudo)}</strong> <span style="font-size:12px;">(${fullUser.age} ${Translator.t('chat.years_old')}, ${orientationSymbol} ${locationDisplay})</span> <span style="font-size:12px;">${languageFlag}</span>`;
641|                         div.style.background = '#a8e3ee';
642|                         div.style.fontStyle = 'italic';
643|                     } else {
644|                         div.innerHTML = `<strong>${escapeHtml(fullUser.pseudo)}</strong> <span style="font-size:12px;">(${fullUser.age} ${Translator.t('chat.years_old')}, ${orientationSymbol} ${locationDisplay})</span> <span style="font-size:12px;">${languageFlag}</span>`;
645|                         if (isBlocked) {
646|                             div.style.background = '#ffcccc';
647|                         }
648| 
649|                         div.onclick = () => {
650|                             sessionStorage.setItem('last_contact', fullUser.pseudo);
651|                             document.querySelectorAll('.user-item').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('current-target'));
652|                             div.classList.add('current-target');
653| 
654|                             currentTarget = fullUser.pseudo;
655|                             currentTargetName = fullUser.pseudo;
656| 
657|                             if (window.blockedUsers.includes(fullUser.pseudo)) {
658|                                 messageInput.disabled = true;
659|                                 sendBtn.disabled = true;
660|                                 messageInput.placeholder = `🔇 ${fullUser.pseudo} ${Translator.t('chat.blocked_placeholder')}`;
661|                             } else {
662|                                 messageInput.disabled = false;
663|                                 sendBtn.disabled = false;
664|                                 messageInput.placeholder = `${Translator.t('chat.message_for')} ${fullUser.pseudo}...`;
665|                             }
666| 
667|                             messageInput.focus();
668|                             clearMessageArea();
669|                             loadHistory(fullUser.pseudo);
670| 
671|                             if (typeof window.showContactInfo === 'function') {
672|                                 window.showContactInfo(
673|                                     fullUser.pseudo,
674|                                     fullUser.age,
675|                                     fullUser.origine,
676|                                     fullUser.gender,
677|                                     fullUser.tendencies,
678|                                     fullUser,
679|                                     fullUser.location_city || fullUser.location_code,
680|                                     fullUser.location_commune || null,
681|                                     fullUser.purpose,
682|                                     fullUser.bio || 'Pas de présentation',
683|                                     window.blockedUsers || []
684|                                 );
685|                             }
686|                         };
687|                     }
688|                     usersDiv.appendChild(div);
689|                 }
690|             }
691| 
692|             // 🔥 Mettre à jour l'ordre des éléments
693|             const items = usersDiv.querySelectorAll('.user-item');
694|             const orderedPseudos = usersList.map(u => u.pseudo);
695|             let needsReorder = false;
696|             for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
697|                 if (items[i].dataset.pseudo !== orderedPseudos[i]) {
698|                     needsReorder = true;
699|                     break;
700|                 }
701|             }
702|             if (needsReorder) {
703|                 for (const pseudo of orderedPseudos) {
704|                     const item = usersDiv.querySelector(`.user-item[data-pseudo="${pseudo}"]`);
705|                     if (item) usersDiv.appendChild(item);
706|                 }
707|             }
708| 
709|             // 🔥 Restaurer la sélection actuelle
710|             const lastContact = sessionStorage.getItem('last_contact');
711|             if (lastContact) {
712|                 const targetItem = usersDiv.querySelector(`.user-item[data-pseudo="${lastContact}"]`);
713|                 if (targetItem && !targetItem.classList.contains('current-target')) {
714|                     document.querySelectorAll('.user-item').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('current-target'));
715|                     targetItem.classList.add('current-target');
716|                 }
717|             }
718| 
719|         } catch (err) {
720|             console.error('❌ Erreur chargement utilisateurs:', err);
721|         }
722|     };

725|     const logoutBtn = document.getElementById('logoutBtn');

797|     function updateUserBadge(warnsCount = 0) {
798|         const userBadge = document.getElementById('userBadge');
799|         if (!userBadge) return;
800| 
801|         // 🔥 Utiliser les nouveaux noms de colonnes
802|         let displayLocation = '?';
803| 
804| 
805|         if (user.location_city && user.location_city !== 'GPS' && user.location_city !== 'Me géolocaliser pour plus de précision') {
806|             displayLocation = user.location_city;
807|         } else {
808|             displayLocation = user.location_code || '?';
809|         }
810| 
811|         if (user.location_commune && user.location_commune !== 'null' &&
812|             user.location_commune !== 'undefined' && user.location_commune !== user.location_city) {
813|             displayLocation = `${user.location_commune} - ${displayLocation}`;
814|         }
815| 
816|         // Si on a une commune précise ET une ville différente, on les combine
817|         if (user.commune && user.commune !== 'null' &&
818|             user.commune !== 'undefined' && user.commune !== user.city) {
819|             displayLocation = `${user.commune} - ${displayLocation}`;
820|         }
821| 
822|         // Afficher le triangle seulement si warnsCount > 0
823|         if (warnsCount > 0) {
824|             const triangleColor = getTriangleColor(warnsCount);
825|             userBadge.innerHTML = `👤 ${escapeHtml(user.pseudo)} | ${escapeHtml(displayLocation)} <span style="color: ${triangleColor}; margin-left: 8px;">⚠️ ${warnsCount}</span>`;
826|         } else {
827|             userBadge.innerHTML = `👤 ${escapeHtml(user.pseudo)} | ${escapeHtml(displayLocation)}`;
828|         }
829|     }

898|         return '#888888';                       // gris

1033|     // ENVOI ET RÉCEPTION DES MESSAGES
1034|     // --------------------------------------------------------------
1035|     socket.on('message sent confirmation', async (data) => {
1036|         displayMessage(data.message, 'sent', null, currentTargetName);
1037|         addToHistory(currentTargetName, data.message, 'sent', user.pseudo, currentTargetName);
1038| 
1039|         try {
1040|             await fetch('/api/messages/save', {
1041|                 method: 'POST',
1042|                 headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
1043|                 body: JSON.stringify({
1044|                     from: user.pseudo,
1045|                     to: currentTargetName,
1046|                     message: data.message
1047|                 })
1048|             });
1049|         } catch (err) {
1050|             console.error('Erreur sauvegarde message:', err);
1051|         }
1052| 
1053|         messageInput.value = '';
1054|         messageInput.focus();
1055|     });
1056| 
1057|     socket.on('private message', async (data) => {
1058|         const { toSocketId, message, fromPseudo } = data;
1059| 
1060|         // Vérifier si le destinataire est connecté
1061|         let targetSocketId = null;
1062|         try {
1063|             const result = await pool.query(
1064|                 'SELECT socket_id FROM users WHERE pseudo = $1 AND socket_id IS NOT NULL',
1065|                 [toSocketId]
1066|             );
1067|             if (result.rows.length > 0) {
1068|                 targetSocketId = result.rows[0].socket_id;
1069|             }
1070|         } catch (err) {
1071|             console.error('❌ Erreur recherche destinataire:', err.message);
1072|             socket.emit('error', { message: 'Erreur lors de l\'envoi du message' });
1073|             return;
1074|         }
1075| 
1076|         // Sauvegarder le message en base de données
1077|         try {
1078|             // 1. Vérifier si une conversation existe déjà entre les deux utilisateurs
1079|             const convResult = await pool.query(
1080|                 `SELECT id FROM conversations 
1081|              WHERE (user1_pseudo = $1 AND user2_pseudo = $2) 
1082|                 OR (user1_pseudo = $2 AND user2_pseudo = $1)`,
1083|                 [fromPseudo, toSocketId]
1084|             );
1085| 
1086|             let conversationId;
1087|             if (convResult.rows.length === 0) {
1088|                 // 2. Créer une nouvelle conversation
1089|                 const newConv = await pool.query(
1090|                     `INSERT INTO conversations (user1_pseudo, user2_pseudo, last_message, last_message_time) 
1091|                  VALUES ($1, $2, $3, NOW()) 
1092|                  RETURNING id`,
1093|                     [fromPseudo, toSocketId, message]
1094|                 );
1095|                 conversationId = newConv.rows[0].id;
1096|                 console.log(`📁 Nouvelle conversation ${conversationId} créée pour ${fromPseudo} ↔ ${toSocketId}`);
1097|             } else {
1098|                 conversationId = convResult.rows[0].id;
1099|                 // 3. Mettre à jour le dernier message
1100|                 await pool.query(
1101|                     `UPDATE conversations SET last_message = $1, last_message_time = NOW() WHERE id = $2`,
1102|                     [message, conversationId]
1103|                 );
1104|             }
1105| 
1106|             // 4. Insérer le message
1107|             await pool.query(
1108|                 `INSERT INTO messages (conversation_id, from_pseudo, to_pseudo, message, created_at) 
1109|              VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, NOW())`,
1110|                 [conversationId, fromPseudo, toSocketId, message]
1111|             );
1112|             console.log(`📦 Message sauvegardé dans la conversation ${conversationId}`);
1113| 
1114|         } catch (err) {
1115|             console.error('❌ Erreur sauvegarde message:', err.message);
1116|             // On continue même si la sauvegarde échoue, pour ne pas bloquer l'envoi
1117|         }
1118| 
1119|         // Si le destinataire est connecté, envoyer le message immédiatement
1120|         if (targetSocketId) {
1121|             io.to(targetSocketId).emit('private message received', {
1122|                 fromPseudo: fromPseudo,
1123|                 fromSocketId: socket.id,
1124|                 message: message,
1125|                 timestamp: new Date()
1126|             });
1127| 
1128|             // Confirmer à l'expéditeur
1129|             socket.emit('message sent confirmation', {
1130|                 message: message,
1131|                 toPseudo: toSocketId,
1132|                 timestamp: new Date()
1133|             });
1134| 
1135|             console.log(`💬 Message de ${fromPseudo} à ${toSocketId}: ${message.substring(0, 30)}...`);
1136|         } else {
1137|             // Le destinataire n'est pas connecté, le message est déjà sauvegardé en base
1138|             console.log(`💤 Message de ${fromPseudo} à ${toSocketId} sauvegardé (destinataire déconnecté)`);
1139|             socket.emit('message sent confirmation', {
1140|                 message: message,
1141|                 toPseudo: toSocketId,
1142|                 timestamp: new Date(),
1143|                 pending: true
1144|             });
1145|         }
1146|     });
1147| 
1148|     socket.on('error', (msg) => {
1149|         console.error('Erreur:', msg);
1150|     });
1151| 
1152|     socket.on('force logout', (data) => {

1234|             const msg = pendingMessages.shift();

1249|                     el.classList.add('current-target');

1276|                 }
1277|             }

1337|     function showPrivatePopup(pseudo) {
1338|         if (document.getElementById('privatePopup')) return;
1339| 
1340|         const chatPanel = document.querySelector('.chat-panel');
1341|         if (!chatPanel) return;
1342| 
1343|         if (getComputedStyle(chatPanel).position !== 'relative') {
1344|             chatPanel.style.position = 'relative';
1345|         }
1346| 
1347|         const inputArea = document.querySelector('.input-area');
1348|         if (!inputArea) return;
1349| 
1350|         const inputRect = inputArea.getBoundingClientRect();
1351|         const chatRect = chatPanel.getBoundingClientRect();
1352|         const topPosition = inputRect.bottom - chatRect.top;
1353| 
1354|         const popup = document.createElement('div');
1355|         popup.id = 'privatePopup';
1356|         popup.style.cssText = `
1357|             position: absolute;
1358|             top: ${topPosition + 5}px;
1359|             left: 0;
1360|             right: 0;
1361|             background: #2c3e50;
1362|             color: white;
1363|             border-radius: 8px;
1364|             padding: 10px 12px;
1365|             z-index: 1000;
1366|             font-family: 'Segoe UI', sans-serif;
1367|             font-size: 12px;
1368|             display: flex;
1369|             justify-content: space-between;
1370|             align-items: center;
1371|             flex-wrap: wrap;
1372|             gap: 8px;
1373|             box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.15);
1374|             opacity: 0;
1375|             transform: translateY(-10px);
1376|             transition: opacity 0.2s ease, transform 0.2s ease;
1377|         `;

1381|             <div style="display: flex; gap: 6px;">
1382|                 <button id="popupAccept" style="background:#27ae60; color:white; border:none; border-radius:4px; padding:4px 10px; cursor:pointer;">✅ Oui</button>
1383|                 <button id="popupDecline" style="background:#e74c3c; color:white; border:none; border-radius:4px; padding:4px 10px; cursor:pointer;">❌ Non</button>
1384|             </div>
1385|         `;
1386|

1391|             popup.style.transform = 'translateY(0)';

1400|             popup.remove();

1526| 
1527|         // VÉRIFICATION 1 : Flag de rechargement
1528|         const reloadFlag = sessionStorage.getItem('meetgay_is_reloading');
1529|         const isReloading = reloadFlag !== null || isPageReloading;
1530|

1532|         let isReload = false;
1533|         try {
1534|             const navEntries = performance.getEntriesByType('navigation');
1535|             if (navEntries && navEntries.length > 0) {
1536|                 isReload = navEntries[0].type === 'reload';
1537|                 console.log(`📊 Type de navigation: ${navEntries[0].type}`);
1538|             }
1539|         } catch (e) {

1541|             if (window.performance && window.performance.navigation) {
1542|                 isReload = window.performance.navigation.type === 1;
1543|                 console.log(`📊 Navigation type (fallback): ${window.performance.navigation.type}`);
1544|             }
1545|         }

1548|         if (isReloading || isReload) {
1549|             console.log(`🔄 IGNORÉ: isReloading=${isReloading}, isReload=${isReload}`);
1550|             return;
1551|         }

1554|         console.log('⬅️ RETOUR ARRIÈRE DÉTECTÉ');
1555|

1558|         const pseudo = storedUser.pseudo || currentUserPseudo;

1563|             return;
1564|         }

1571|         setTimeout(() => navigator.sendBeacon('/api/delete-user', data), 50);
1572|         setTimeout(() => navigator.sendBeacon('/api/messages/delete-user', data), 100);

1587|     });

# FILE: public\js\common.js

1| ﻿// ========== COMMON - MeetGay ==========
2| // Fonctions et détecteurs partagés entre les pages (login, chat, etc.)
3| 
4| (function () {
5|     // ========== 1. Détection de la résolution d'écran ==========
6|     function detectResolution() {
7|         const width = window.innerWidth;
8|         const height = window.innerHeight;
9|         const resolution = `${width}x${height}`;
10|         sessionStorage.setItem('screen_resolution', resolution);
11|         console.log(`📱 Résolution détectée : ${resolution}`);
12|         return resolution;
13|     }
14| 
15|     // ========== 2. Échapper le HTML (sécurité) ==========
16|     function escapeHtml(str) {
17|         if (!str) return '';
18|         return str.replace(/[&<>]/g, (m) => {
19|             if (m === '&') return '&amp;';
20|             if (m === '<') return '&lt;';
21|             if (m === '>') return '&gt;';
22|             return m;
23|         });
24|     }
25| 
26|     // ========== 3. Formater l'heure (HH:MM) ==========
27|     function formatTime(date) {
28|         if (!date) return '';
29|         const d = new Date(date);
30|         return `${d.getHours().toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${d.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
31|     }
32| 
33|     // ========== 4. Exécuter la détection au chargement ==========
34|     if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
35|         document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', detectResolution);
36|     } else {
37|         detectResolution();
38|     }
39| 
40|     // ========== 5. Exposer les fonctions globalement ==========
41|     window.common = {
42|         escapeHtml: escapeHtml,
43|         formatTime: formatTime,
44|         detectResolution: detectResolution,
45|         getResolution: () => sessionStorage.getItem('screen_resolution')
46|     };
47| 
48|     // Fonction pour afficher la localisation de manière cohérente (utilisée par le badge ET la liste des connectés)
49|     window.getDisplayLocation = function (userData) {
50|         if (!userData) return '?';
51| 
52|         let locationCity = userData.locationCity || userData.location_city || null;
53|         let locationCommune = userData.locationCommune || userData.location_commune || null;
54|         let locationCode = userData.locationCode || userData.location_code || '';
55| 
56|         // Si on a une commune précise différente de la ville, on combine
57|         if (locationCommune && locationCommune !== 'null' && locationCommune !== 'undefined' && locationCommune !== locationCity) {
58|             return `${locationCommune} - ${locationCity}`;
59|         }
60| 
61|         // Sinon on affiche juste la ville
62|         if (locationCity && locationCity !== 'null' && locationCity !== 'undefined') {
63|             return locationCity;
64|         }
65| 
66|         // Fallback : code de la liste déroulante
67|         if (locationCode && locationCode !== 'null' && locationCode !== 'undefined' && locationCode !== 'GPS') {
68|             return locationCode;
69|         }
70| 
71|         return '?';
72|     };
73| })();

1| ﻿import FingerprintJS from '@fingerprintjs/fingerprintjs';
2| 
3| (async () => {
4|     try {
5|         const fp = await FingerprintJS.load();
6|         const result = await fp.get();
7|         const visitorId = result.visitorId;
8|         sessionStorage.setItem('fingerprint', visitorId);
9|         window.currentFingerprint = visitorId;
10|         console.log('🔒 Fingerprint généré:', visitorId);
11|     } catch (err) {
12|         console.error('❌ Erreur fingerprint:', err);
13|         // Fallback temporaire (évite de bloquer l'utilisateur)
14|         const fallback = 'temp_' + Date.now();
15|         sessionStorage.setItem('fingerprint', fallback);
16|         window.currentFingerprint = fallback;
17|     }
18| })();

18| // CHARGEMENT DYNAMIQUE DE GOOGLE MAPS
19| // ============================================================
20| 
21| let googleMapsLoaded = false;
22| 
23| function loadGoogleMapsAPI() {
24|     return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
25|         // Si déjà chargé, résoudre immédiatement
26|         if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.maps) {
27|             googleMapsLoaded = true;
28|             resolve();
29|             return;
30|         }
31| 
32|         // Récupérer la clé API depuis le serveur
33|         fetch('/api/config/google-maps-key')
34|             .then(response => response.json())
35|             .then(data => {
36|                 if (!data.apiKey) {
37|                     reject(new Error('Clé API non trouvée'));
38|                     return;
39|                 }
40| 
41|                 // Créer le script
42|                 const script = document.createElement('script');
43|                 script.src = `https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/js?key=${data.apiKey}&libraries=places&language=fr&region=BE`;
44|                 script.async = true;
45|                 script.defer = true;
46| 
47|                 script.onload = () => {
48|                     googleMapsLoaded = true;
49|                     console.log('✅ Google Maps chargé dynamiquement');
50|                     resolve();
51|                 };
52| 
53|                 script.onerror = () => {
54|                     reject(new Error('Erreur de chargement de Google Maps'));
55|                 };
56| 
57|                 document.head.appendChild(script);
58|             })
59|             .catch(error => {
60|                 reject(error);
61|             });
62|     });
63| }

166|     // 2. Fonction de géolocalisation
167|     function startGeolocation() {
168|         if (!navigator.geolocation) {
169|             gpsStatus.innerHTML = Translator.t('geolocation.error_not_supported');
170|             // Afficher le bouton "Ma localité n'est pas bonne"
171|             const switchZone = document.getElementById('switchToManualZone');
172|             if (switchZone) {
173|                 switchZone.style.display = 'block';
174|             }
175|             return;
176|         }
177| 
178|         navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
179|             // Succès
180|             function (position) {
181|                 detectedLocationLat = position.coords.latitude;
182|                 detectedLocationLng = position.coords.longitude;
183| 
184|                 gpsStatus.innerHTML = '📍 Position trouvée ! Je cherche le nom de la ville...';
185| 
186|                 // Appel à Nominatim pour avoir le nom de la ville
187|                 getCityName(detectedLocationLat, detectedLocationLng);
188| 
189|             },
190|             // Erreur
191|             function (error) {
192|                 let msg = Translator.t('geolocation.error_default');
193|                 switch (error.code) {
194|                     case error.PERMISSION_DENIED:
195|                         msg = Translator.t('geolocation.error_permission_denied');
196|                         break;
197|                     case error.POSITION_UNAVAILABLE:
198|                         msg = Translator.t('geolocation.error_position_unavailable');
199|                         break;
200|                     case error.TIMEOUT:
201|                         msg = Translator.t('geolocation.error_timeout');
202|                         break;
203|                 }
204|                 gpsStatus.innerHTML = msg;
205| 
206|                 // Afficher le bouton "Ma localité n'est pas bonne" en cas d'erreur
207|                 const switchZone = document.getElementById('switchToManualZone');
208|                 if (switchZone) {
209|                     switchZone.style.display = 'block';
210|                 }
211|             },
212|             {
213|                 enableHighAccuracy: true,
214|                 timeout: 10000,
215|                 maximumAge: 0
216|             }
217|         );
218|     }
219| 
220|     function getCityName(lat, lng) {
221|         const url = `/api/geocode?lat=${lat}&lng=${lng}`;
222| 
223|         fetch(url)
224|             .then(response => response.json())
225|             .then(data => {
226|                 console.log('📡 Réponse Google Maps:', data);
227| 
228|                 if (data.status === 'OK' && data.results.length > 0) {
229|                     // On prend le premier résultat (le plus précis)
230|                     // === NOUVEAU CODE ===
231|                     // 🔥 On prend le résultat le PLUS PRÉCIS (viewport le plus petit)
232|                     const sortedResults = [...data.results].sort((a, b) => {
233|                         const sizeA = (a.geometry.viewport.northeast.lat - a.geometry.viewport.southwest.lat) *
234|                             (a.geometry.viewport.northeast.lng - a.geometry.viewport.southwest.lng);
235|                         const sizeB = (b.geometry.viewport.northeast.lat - b.geometry.viewport.southwest.lat) *
236|                             (b.geometry.viewport.northeast.lng - b.geometry.viewport.southwest.lng);
237|                         return sizeA - sizeB;
238|                     });
239|                     const result = sortedResults[0];
240|                     console.log('📍 Résultat le PLUS PRÉCIS sélectionné:', result.formatted_address);
241| 
242|                     // --- RÉCUPÉRATION DES COMPOSANTS ---
243|                     const components = result.address_components;
244|                     console.log('📡 COMPOSANTS COMPLETS:', components.map(c => ({ long_name: c.long_name, types: c.types })));
245| 
246|                     // --- RÉCUPÉRATION DES COMPOSANTS ---
247|                     const trouver = (type) => components.find(c => c.types.includes(type))?.long_name;
248|                     // ... le reste du code
249| 
250|                     const locality = trouver("locality") || trouver("postal_town");
251|                     const sublocality = trouver("sublocality") || trouver("sublocality_level_1") || trouver("neighborhood");
252|                     const postalCode = trouver("postal_code");
253|                     const province = trouver("administrative_area_level_2") || trouver("administrative_area_level_3");
254|                     const region = trouver("administrative_area_level_1");
255|                     const country = trouver("country");
256|                     const neighborhood = trouver("neighborhood") || sublocality;
257| 
258|                     // 🔥 NOUVEAU : Récupérer les localités du code postal
259|                     const postcodeLocalities = data.results.find(r => r.types.includes("postal_code"))?.postcode_localities || [];
260| 
261|                     console.log('📍 Locality:', locality);
262|                     console.log('📍 Sublocality:', sublocality);
263|                     console.log('📍 Postcode Localities:', postcodeLocalities);
264| 
265|                     // --- DÉTERMINATION DE LA COMMUNE PRÉCISE ---
266|                     let communePrecise = locality;
267| 
268|                     // Priorité 1: sublocality (Pamel, Borchtlombeek, Strijtem)
269|                     if (sublocality && sublocality !== locality) {
270|                         communePrecise = sublocality;
271|                     }
272|                     // Priorité 2: postcode_localities (si sublocality n'existe pas)
273|                     else if (postcodeLocalities.length > 0 && postcodeLocalities[0] !== locality) {
274|                         communePrecise = postcodeLocalities[0];
275|                     }
276| 
277|                     console.log('✅ Commune précise trouvée:', communePrecise);
278| 
279|                     // --- AFFICHAGE ---
280|                     gpsStatus.style.display = 'none';
281|                     gpsResult.style.display = 'block';
282|                     detectedCity.textContent = locality;
283|                     document.getElementById('detectedCounty').textContent = communePrecise;
284| 
285|                     // OPTION 1 : juste la ville
286|                     document.getElementById('choiceRegionLabel').textContent = locality;
287| 
288|                     // Afficher le bouton "Ma localité n'est pas bonne"
289|                     const switchZone = document.getElementById('switchToManualZone');
290|                     if (switchZone) {
291|                         switchZone.style.display = 'block';
292|                     }
293| 
294|                     // OPTION 2 : commune précise + ville (si différente)
295|                     let option2 = locality;
296|                     if (communePrecise && communePrecise !== locality) {
297|                         option2 = `${communePrecise} - ${locality}`;
298|                     } else if (detectedLocationCountry) {
299|                         option2 = `${locality} - ${detectedLocationCountry}`;
300|                     }
301|                     document.getElementById('choicePreciseLabel').textContent = option2;
302| 
303|                     // Réinitialiser les radios
304|                     document.querySelectorAll('input[name="locationChoice"]').forEach(el => el.checked = false);
305|                     document.getElementById('gpsConsent').checked = false;
306|                     document.getElementById('gpsConfirm').disabled = true;
307|                     gpsConsent.disabled = false;
308| 
309|                     // Stocker pour confirmation
310|                     detectedLocationCity = locality;
311|                     detectedLocationCommune = communePrecise;
312|                     detectedLocationPostalCode = postalCode || '';
313|                     detectedLocationProvince = province || '';
314|                     detectedLocationRegion = region || '';
315|                     detectedLocationCountry = country || '';
316|                     detectedLocationNeighborhood = sublocality || neighborhood || '';
317|                     detectedLocationPostcodeLocalities = postcodeLocalities.join(', ') || '';
318|                     detectedLocationLat = lat;
319|                     detectedLocationLng = lng;
320| 
321|                     // Message informatif
322|                     const infoMsg = document.createElement('div');
323|                     infoMsg.style.cssText = 'font-size: 0.8em; color: #666; margin-top: 5px;';
324|                     infoMsg.innerHTML = `
325|                     ℹ️ <strong>Option 1:</strong> ${locality}<br>
326|                     ℹ️ <strong>Option 2:</strong> ${option2}
327|                 `;
328|                     const oldMsg = document.querySelector('.gps-info-msg');
329|                     if (oldMsg) oldMsg.remove();
330|                     infoMsg.className = 'gps-info-msg';
331|                     document.querySelector('#gpsResult')?.appendChild(infoMsg);
332| 
333|                 } else {
334|                     gpsStatus.innerHTML = '❌ Erreur Google Maps';
335|                 }
336|             })
337|             .catch(error => {
338|                 gpsStatus.innerHTML = '❌ Erreur';
339|                 console.error(error);
340|             });
341|     }
342| 
343|     // 4. Case à cocher : activer/désactiver le bouton Confirmer
344|     gpsConsent.addEventListener('change', function () {
345|         const radioSelected = document.querySelector('input[name="locationChoice"]:checked') !== null;
346|         gpsConfirm.disabled = !(this.checked && radioSelected);
347|     });

351|         radio.addEventListener('change', function () {
352|             const consentChecked = gpsConsent.checked;
353|             gpsConfirm.disabled = !(consentChecked && this.checked);
354|         });

358|     gpsConfirm.addEventListener('click', function () {
359|         if (!detectedLocationCity) {
360|             alert('Aucune ville détectée. Réessayez.');
361|             return;
362|         }
363| 
364|         const selectedChoice = document.querySelector('input[name="locationChoice"]:checked');
365|         if (!selectedChoice) {
366|             alert('Veuillez choisir comment afficher votre localité.');
367|             return;
368|         }
369| 
370|         let locationCity = '';
371|         let locationCommune = null;
372| 
373|         // Créer les champs cachés pour toutes les données de localisation
374|         function createHiddenField(id, value) {
375|             let field = document.getElementById(id);
376|             if (!field) {
377|                 field = document.createElement('input');
378|                 field.type = 'hidden';
379|                 field.id = id;
380|                 field.name = id.replace('_hidden', '');
381|                 document.getElementById('loginForm').appendChild(field);
382|             }
383|             field.value = value || '';
384|         }
385| 
386| 
387|         if (selectedChoice.value === 'region_only') {
388|             locationCity = detectedLocationCity;
389|             locationCommune = null;
390|         } else {
391|             if (detectedLocationCommune && detectedLocationCommune !== detectedLocationCity) {
392|                 locationCity = detectedLocationCity;
393|                 locationCommune = detectedLocationCommune;
394|             } else {
395|                 locationCity = detectedLocationCity;
396|                 locationCommune = null;
397|             }
398|         }
399| 
400|         // Stocker toutes les données dans des champs cachés
401|         createHiddenField('location_city_hidden', locationCity);
402|         createHiddenField('location_commune_hidden', locationCommune);
403|         createHiddenField('location_postal_code_hidden', detectedLocationPostalCode);
404|         createHiddenField('location_province_hidden', detectedLocationProvince);
405|         createHiddenField('location_region_hidden', detectedLocationRegion);
406|         createHiddenField('location_country_hidden', detectedLocationCountry);
407|         createHiddenField('location_neighborhood_hidden', detectedLocationNeighborhood);
408|         createHiddenField('location_postcode_localities_hidden', detectedLocationPostcodeLocalities);
409|         createHiddenField('location_sublocality_level_1_hidden', detectedLocationSublocalityLevel1);
410|         createHiddenField('location_sublocality_level_2_hidden', detectedLocationSublocalityLevel2);
411|         createHiddenField('location_sublocality_level_3_hidden', detectedLocationSublocalityLevel3);
412|         createHiddenField('location_sublocality_level_4_hidden', detectedLocationSublocalityLevel4);
413|         createHiddenField('location_sublocality_level_5_hidden', detectedLocationSublocalityLevel5);
414|         createHiddenField('location_admin_level_3_hidden', detectedLocationAdminLevel3);
415|         createHiddenField('location_admin_level_4_hidden', detectedLocationAdminLevel4);
416|         createHiddenField('location_admin_level_5_hidden', detectedLocationAdminLevel5);
417|         createHiddenField('location_admin_level_6_hidden', detectedLocationAdminLevel6);
418|         createHiddenField('location_admin_level_7_hidden', detectedLocationAdminLevel7);
419|         createHiddenField('location_colloquial_area_hidden', detectedLocationColloquialArea);
420|         createHiddenField('location_intersection_hidden', detectedLocationIntersection);
421|         createHiddenField('location_lat_hidden', detectedLocationLat);
422|         createHiddenField('location_lng_hidden', detectedLocationLng);
423| 
424|         // 🔥 DEBUG - Vérifier les valeurs
425|         console.log('📍 Sauvegarde GPS - locationCity:', locationCity);
426|         console.log('📍 Sauvegarde GPS - locationCommune:', locationCommune);
427|

430|         // On crée une option pour la ville si elle n'existe pas
431|         const locationSelect = document.getElementById('locationSelect');
432|         let optionExists = false;
433|         if (locationSelect && locationSelect.options) {
434|             for (let opt of locationSelect.options) {
435|                 if (opt.value === locationCity) {
436|                     optionExists = true;
437|                     break;
438|                 }
439|             }
440|         }
441|         if (!optionExists) {
442|             const newOption = document.createElement('option');
443|             newOption.value = locationCity;
444|             newOption.textContent = locationCity;
445|             locationSelect.appendChild(newOption);
446|         }
447|         locationSelect.value = locationCity;
448| 
449|         const displayText = locationCommune ? `${locationCommune} - ${locationCity}` : locationCity;
450| 
451|         gpsZone.innerHTML = `
452|         <div style="padding: 10px; background: #e8f5e9; border-radius: 5px; color: #2e7d32;">
453|             ✅ Localisation confirmée : <strong>${locationCity}</strong>
454|             <br><small>Votre profil affichera : ${displayText}</small>
455|         </div>
456|     `;
457|     });

512|     // ===== INITIALISATION DE L'AUTOCOMPLÉTION =====
513|     async function initAutocomplete() {
514|         try {
515|             if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.maps) {
516|                 console.log('⏳ Google Maps non chargé, réessai dans 500ms');
517|                 setTimeout(initAutocomplete, 500);
518|                 return;
519|             }
520| 
521|             const { AutocompleteSuggestion } = await google.maps.importLibrary("places");
522|             autocompleteService = AutocompleteSuggestion;
523|             sessionToken = new google.maps.places.AutocompleteSessionToken();
524|             console.log('✅ Autocomplétion initialisée (Places New)');
525|         } catch (err) {
526|             console.error('❌ Erreur init autocomplete:', err);
527|         }
528|     }
